65 active windows · 5%–60% · 20/50/30 omission preference

Published as a separate research release: 551 approved variants. Held variants are excluded. The human pilot’s conditions, passages, QAs and assignments are unchanged. The target mix counts deletion occurrences, pooled across the selected windows separately at each dose. Dose counts deleted clean Chinese content characters, with ±2 percentage points allowed in every window. The published 15% and 30% versions are retained exactly. The 30%, 40%, 50% and 60% versions keep every earlier deletion; other doses are selected independently.

Existing reviewed units retain their classifications; multi-clause spans count as clause-level deletion occurrences in the three-category mix. New combinations receive the unchanged five-check bilingual review, without the QA, answer key, desired mix, or prior verdicts.

Required checks: information omitted, substantive content removed, fluent Chinese, deletion only, and valid in the displayed context. Semantic distortion, ambiguous references, and recoverability are recorded as nonblocking flags. A flagged pass meets all five checks; a failed or uncertain check remains unavailable for publication.

1 Kings 13:6–7 · 60%Needs review — failed checkfluent = noThe edits remove the man of God’s identity as addressee, his relationship to God, the king as beneficiary, the requested restoration, the actual intercession and healing, accompaniment home, and the reward. These are substantive losses supported by the bilingual source. Fluency fails at “和从前一样。”: with both intervening narrative clauses deleted and the preceding quotation already closed, this standalone comparison lacks an established subject or state to compare. It is a dangling remnant of the healing report. The retained “你” no longer has an identified addressee, hence ambiguous_reference. “请回家” changes the invitation to accompany the king into a nonspecific request to return home, hence other_meaning_changed. The supplied result follows the listed deletions without additions or reordering.
1 Kings 13:26–30 · 50%Needs review — failed checkfluent = noSubstantive losses include the prophet’s bringing the man back and hearing the report, the LORD’s command and causal role in the death, fulfillment of his word, the sons’ identity, the roadside location, the animals’ position, ownership of the city and tomb, and the communal brotherly lament. Fluency fails at the window’s opening, “就说：“这是那违背命令的神人。狮子将他咬死。””: the reporting clause has neither an established speaker nor a preceding event anchoring “就”. The later mention of the old prophet suggests an attribution but leaves this opening dangling. ambiguous_reference also applies to “他们”, whose plural antecedent has been deleted. other_meaning_changed: the retained standing clause now describes the prophet, and the final lament becomes his individual action rather than the crowd’s. recoverable_from_context: the corpse’s identity and the mourning target remain inferable. The edits are deletion-only, and the identified losses are grounded in the supplied source.
1 Kings 13:26–30 · 60%Needs review — failed checkfluent = noSubstantive losses include the prophet’s earlier retrieval of the man and hearing the report, the LORD’s command, his delivering the man to the lion and fulfillment of his word, the sons and their compliance, the roadside location, the animals standing beside the body, the donkey’s remaining unharmed, transport on the donkey, ownership of the city and tomb, and the communal brotherly lament. Fluency fails at “就说：“这是那违背命令的神人。狮子将他咬死。””: the window begins with an unanchored reporting clause, without an established speaker or preceding event for “就”. ambiguous_reference marks this unattached speaker; the subsequent old prophet is only an inferred attribution. other_meaning_changed: the prophet now appears to stand beside the body and to perform the final lament individually. recoverable_from_context: the corpse’s identity and the mourning target remain inferable. Removing both “既” and its paired clause leaves the later sentence grammatical. All edits are listed deletions, and the losses are supported by the bilingual window.

Clean passage

13:1　忽然，耶罗波安站在祭坛旁烧香的时候，有一个神人奉耶和华的话，从犹大来到伯特利。

13:2　他奉耶和华的话向祭坛呼喊说：“祭坛哪，祭坛哪！耶和华如此说：‘大卫家必生一个儿子，名叫约西亚。他必在你上面，将那些在你上面烧香的丘坛祭司献为祭，也必在你上面焚烧人的骨头。’”

13:3　当天，神人给了一个预兆，说：“这是耶和华所说的预兆：‘这祭坛必裂开，坛上的灰必倾撒出来。’”

However, “他们” now refers to unnamed recipients rather than explicitly identified sons, leaving their identity unspecified. The changes are exactly the listed deletions and are grounded in the source.

删去了麻风病持续到死亡的明确期限、所记载内容为“乌西雅其余的事迹”的限定，以及墓地靠近列祖的位置关系，均涉及实质信息。全文仍可作合乎语法的理解：“自始至终都由亚摩斯的儿子以赛亚先知记载下来”可以承接前文事件，省略被记载的对象。不过，这一对象不再明确，且紧接约坦治理国民的叙述，可能被理解为记录前述事件或约坦的活动，因此标记指代歧义及可能的意义变化。记录行为本身并未被删除。所有改动均为所列删除，其他保留内容及所判定的信息损失均有双语原文支持。

Clean passage

6:32　那时，以利沙正坐在自己家中，长老们也与他同坐。王派了一个使者先去，但使者还没到，以利沙就对长老们说：“你们看见了吗？这个凶手派人来砍我的头！你们留意，使者来的时候，就关上门，把他挡在外面。在他后面，不是有他主人的脚步声吗？”

6:33　以利沙还在与他们说话的时候，使者就下到他那里。王说：“这灾祸是从耶和华来的，我为什么还要等候耶和华呢？”

Acts 19:17–20

Clean passage

19:17　这事传遍了住在以弗所的所有犹太人和希腊人，众人都心生惧怕。因此，主耶稣的名大受尊崇。

19:18　许多已经信主的人这时也走上前来，承认并公开自己所做的事。

19:19　还有不少曾经行邪术的人，把自己的书带来，当众焚烧。他们计算这些书的价值，总共是五万德拉克马。

19:20　这样，主的道就大有能力地不断传开，日益兴旺。

The result is terse but grammatical. ambiguous_reference flags the unidentified confessors and book-bringers. other_meaning_changed flags the broadened Jewish audience, apparent transfer of subsequent actions to the crowd, and confession replacing the honoring of Jesus as the stated consequence of fear. recoverable_from_context concerns the books’ implied monetary value, not the deleted calculation event. These distortions are nonblocking.

17:1　以法莲山地有一个人，名叫米迦。

17:2　他说：“我听见你为此发了咒诅。银子就在我这里，是我拿的。”他母亲说：“愿我儿蒙耶和华赐福！”

The edit removes the amount (1,100 shekels), the explicit statement that the silver was taken from the mother, and the explicit identification of her as the addressee. These are substantive details, although Micah’s possession and admission of taking the silver remain. The entire edited window is grammatical: “为此” can refer implicitly to the taking mentioned afterward. Its referent and the identity of “你” are less explicit, hence ambiguous_reference. The mother’s reply makes her role as addressee inferable, hence recoverable_from_context, but does not recover the amount or explicitly establish that the silver was taken from her. The changes consist solely of the listed deletions, and the assessment is supported by the bilingual window.

17:13　米迦说：“耶和华必恩待我，因为有一个利未人作了祭司。”

Substantive losses include the search for somewhere to settle, the invitation to serve as father and priest, clothing as compensation, entering, the son-like relationship, actual residence, and Micah's statement of present knowledge.
